# id|mode|want|category|record_type|tags|portal|name|name_ja|url|probe|description|opts
#
# Batch 41 asked each of these hosts for ONE collection, wp/v2/posts. This beat
# asks for what the host's OWN route table at /wp-json advertises, and keeps only
# the collections that answered with records. Measured 2026-10-12:
#   461 hosts asked for their route table; 433 answered.
#   6,726 collection routes advertised, 1,570 distinct.
#   After dropping editor/plugin machinery and taxonomy term lists, 2,866 were
#   probed and 2,567 returned >=1 record (216 EMPTY, 59 HTTP error, 13 transport,
#   11 not-a-list).
# Every count in a description below is what that collection's own response held.
# wp/v2/media is excluded on measurement, not taste -- see the beat generator's
# header and docs/rejected-sources-batch42.media.tsv.
